- **latent-dimension-sensitivity-analysis**: Use when a VASC (or similar deep variational autoencoder) latent representation must be validated for robustness: you have labeled scRNA-seq data (e.g., Pollen), need to pick a latent dimension, and must show NMI/ARI stability across sampling fractions and read depths. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/latent-dimension-sensitivity-analysis))
- **latent-dimension-traversal-pseudocell-generation**: Use when a trained scMM model's latent space needs biological interpretation: to link individual latent dimensions to gene expression programs, surface protein levels, or chromatin accessibility motifs from CITE-seq/Atlas-seq multimodal single-cell data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/latent-dimension-traversal-pseudocell-generation))
- **latent-dimensionality-sweep-analysis**: Use when you have denoised single-cell expression matrices plus known phenotype labels and need to compare methods by cluster resolution, sweeping latent-space dimensions and reporting mean silhouette scores per method and dimension. - **latent-dirichlet-allocation-inference**: Use when you have a scRNA-seq count matrix (cells x genes) and want to decompose it into interpretable latent biological function topics via a penalized LDA topic model in R. - **latent-dirichlet-allocation-topic-inference**: Use when you have a preprocessed corpus of mass spectrometry spectra converted to bag-of-fragments format (with neutral losses extracted and noise filtered), and you seek to discover recurring fragmentation patterns or substructures that characterize multiple spectra without prior knowledge of the. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/latent-dirichlet-allocation-topic-inference) · 2 copies)
- **latent-dirichlet-allocation-topic-modeling**: Use when you have a corpus of MS2 fragmentation spectra (e.g., MGF from GNPS or Mass Bank) and want unsupervised extraction of recurring molecular substructures without prior compound identification, or when testing that a spectral dataset decomposes into a target number of motifs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/latent-dirichlet-allocation-topic-modeling))
- **latent-dirichlet-allocation-topic-modeling**: Use when ST pixels are multi-cellular (e.g. Visium, DBiT-seq, Slide-seq) and no suitable scRNA-seq reference exists, or cell-type proportions and marker-enriched transcriptional profiles must be inferred in an unsupervised manner.
